# Runtime Data Ledger rev 3 - group ui-input

Part of the [runtime data ledger, rev 3](../RUNTIME-DATA-LEDGER.md) (the index: vocabularies, totals, entity model, kernel features, decisions). **Tree:** `D:/wt/joltab`, `merge/ke16-into-ecsnative` @ `d11962a9`. **Rows:** 205, of which **83 active** and **122 SUPERSEDED** by the ui-lane (kept below, marked, not counted, not in the TSV); 48 non_rows (kept in the group JSON ledger).

Active forms: diagnostics 44, resource-column 23, system-scratch 14, event 2. Active classes: R 8, B 31, D 44. Crates: boyko_input 83. Rungs: R4 8, R6 75.

## Contents

- [Findings](#findings)
- [Kernel features](#kernel-features)
- [Primitives refuted](#primitives-refuted)
- [Plan conflicts](#plan-conflicts)
- [Rows](#rows)

## Findings

- While hot-reload is on, `ui_hot_reload_system` allocates at runtime every 250 ms: `std::fs::metadata` -> `maybe_verbatim` -> a `Vec<u16>` (row reload/system.rs:123). Only a counting or denying `#[global_allocator]` can see it. This was derived from source, not measured. **[rev 2]** Superseded by the ui-lane row reload/system.rs:123, decided resource-column (UL-D2, in-house GetFileAttributesExW on a pre-encoded path).
- The `Arc<Mutex>` sinks at reload/system.rs:167/168 need no storage at all, because `run_system` already returns the system's output.
- UiTreeView copies 22 components per widget, plus the Children relation. It exists only because a FunctionSystem cannot read arbitrary components alongside ResMut + Commands. The missing piece is a capability (KF-28), not a data structure.

## Primitives refuted

- **ScratchStack&lt;T: Copy> (LIFO segmented stack over one reservation)**
  - replaced by: system-scratch on ONE ScratchColumn per role with a segment mark held in the recursion frame and truncate-on-return
  - evidence: boyko_ui/src/layout.rs:540 "let child_buf = &mut scratch.child_pool[depth];"; boyko_ui/src/layout.rs:856 "scratch.child_idx_pool[depth].push(child_idx);"; boyko_ui/src/layout.rs:694 "scratch.child_index.extend_from_slice(&lane);"; boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/views.rs:208 "pub fn truncate(&mut self, new_len: usize) {"; boyko_ui/src/reload/reconcile.rs:358 "let mut child_ids = Vec::new();"
  - note: Every depth-pool slot is touched only inside its own depth's measure_node activation (filled at entry, appended after child returns, copied to the flat arena before return, never read by the position pass), and a reconcile callee's child_ids is dropped unread: strictly LIFO, so the segment is just [mark, len) of a flat column. Address stability is not even required (all access is by index). The two stacks (row boyko_ui/src/reload/tree_view.rs:109, row boyko_ui/src/text/parser.rs:61) are single-call LIFOs: push/truncate.
  - rows: boyko_ui/src/resources.rs:216; boyko_ui/src/resources.rs:218; boyko_ui/src/resources.rs:226; boyko_ui/src/reload/reconcile.rs:272; boyko_ui/src/reload/reconcile.rs:273; boyko_ui/src/reload/reconcile.rs:300; boyko_ui/src/reload/reconcile.rs:358; boyko_ui/src/reload/tree_view.rs:109; boyko_ui/src/text/parser.rs:61
- **CsrColumn&lt;T: Copy> (values column + per-owner ranges, in-place range insert)**
  - replaced by: resource-column = two Resource-owned ScratchColumns (values, (start,len) ranges) for InputMap and fonts; system-scratch column pairs for the parse tree; the kernel Children relation for LiveNode::children
  - evidence: boyko_input/src/action/map.rs:134 "bindings: Box&lt;[BindSpec]>,"; boyko_input/src/action/map.rs:136 "ranges: Box&lt;[(u32, u32)]>,"; boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/views.rs:141 "pub fn push(&mut self, value: T) -> u32"; boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/views.rs:109 "pub fn as_mut_slice(&mut self) -> &mut [T] {"
  - note: The owners are action indices and font ids (not entities), so relation / dense-component-keyed-by-owner do not apply; two resource columns are the ECS form. The rebind insert is push + copy_within + range fix-up on the existing view API; the builder's out-of-order arrival is a counting sort by action.
  - rows: boyko_input/src/action/map.rs:134/136/242/275; boyko_ui/src/text/font.rs:33/38/41; boyko_ui/src/text/ast.rs:73/75; boyko_ui/src/reload/tree_view.rs:55
- **LoadArena (phase-reset bump arena for load/reload transients)**
  - replaced by: system-scratch: typed ScratchColumns owned by the loader system (UiHotReload / the startup systems), strings as (offset, len) spans into the held source ScratchColumn&lt;u8>
  - evidence: boyko_ui/src/text/parser.rs:310 "name: name.to_string(),"; boyko_ui/src/text/parser.rs:311 "body: body.to_string(),"; boyko_ui/src/plugin.rs:105 "app.add_startup_system(move \|mut cmds: Commands, mut hot: ResMut&lt;UiHotReload>\| {"
  - note: The load and reload already run inside ECS systems, so their working set is system-scratch by definition. The heterogeneous-bump argument fails on inspection: the working set is about six element types (ParsedNode, component spans, u32 children, u8 source, (UiName, Entity), Entity), one column each; the strings are substrings of the source and need no copy. The reset-per-phase cadence is clear() on those columns.
  - rows: every B row with ecs_form system-scratch under boyko_ui/src/text and boyko_ui/src/reload, plus the boyko_input .keys load rows
- **ByteSink (VmReservation-backed core::fmt::Write sink)**
  - replaced by: system-scratch on ScratchColumn&lt;u8> + KF6 (fmt::Write on its build view)
  - evidence: boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/views.rs:164 "pub fn extend_from_slice(&mut self, values: &[T])"
  - note: A trait impl on the existing column, not a second byte primitive. The design space's HeapString (D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:217 "pub struct HeapString(HeapVec&lt;u8>);") is the same need; see PC2.
  - rows: boyko_input/src/persist/writer.rs:30/57/58; boyko_input/src/persist/keyname.rs:215/249; boyko_ui/src/text/serialize.rs:53; boyko_ui/src/reload/reconcile.rs:757
- **DiagArena (diagnostic records + message bytes)**
  - replaced by: boyko_log structured records (codes + tagged args, no String) for emission, and a kernel event (UiParseDiagnostic { line, col, code }) for any consumer of the retained report
  - evidence: boyko_ui/src/text/report.rs:27 "pub errors: Vec&lt;(usize, u16, String)>,"; D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:361 "error types carry codes (`boyko_log/src/codes.rs` already exists)"
  - note: The engine already has one diagnostics system with a code registry and an allocation-free tagged payload; a UI-local arena would be a second one.
  - rows: all 61 D rows
- **public, ComponentId-free VmColumn&lt;T: Copy> / ScratchColumn constructor**
  - replaced by: KF1 (ScratchColumn::for_type) - VmColumn stays kernel-private
  - evidence: boyko_ecs/src/ecs/memory/vm_column.rs:80 "pub(crate) struct VmColumn&lt;T: Copy> {"; boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/scratch_column.rs:43 "pub struct ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy> {"
  - note: Exporting VmColumn would put a second typed column beside ComponentPool, which the owner rejected; ScratchColumn is already ComponentPool-backed and public. Consistent with the render group's ecsform refutation of the same export.
  - rows: every F and R row that the verified file sent to Resource+VmColumn or ScratchColumn
- **"inline bounded array in the Resource (no heap)" (ActionState, action names) and "plain field on UiHotReload" (reload sinks)**
  - replaced by: not primitives: the first is resource-column in the Resource's own kernel storage; the second is nothing at all, because run_system already returns the system's output
  - evidence: boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/ecs_master/system_api.rs:111 "pub fn run_system&lt;F, M, Out>(&mut self, system: F) -> Out"; boyko_input/tests/i4_ecs_integration.rs:304 "let jumped = world.run_system(\|state: Res&lt;ActionState&lt;GameplayAction>>\| {"
  - note: Recorded so the design does not count them as new memory primitives.
  - rows: boyko_input/src/action/state.rs:61-67/92-95; boyko_input/src/action/names.rs:42/54/56; boyko_ui/src/reload/system.rs:167/168

## Plan conflicts

- **PC1** *rows:* all UI frame lanes: boyko_ui/src/resources.rs:216-254, world/pick.rs:110-120, interaction/focus.rs:117-136, binding/bind_system.rs:46-50, widgets.rs:73-75 *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:343 "`FrameVec` on the `FrameArena` (clear+refill per frame is exactly the frame class)" *plan_b:* this ledger: system-scratch on kernel ScratchColumn (ComponentPool-backed, already carrying physics scratch) *why_flagged:* The owner rejected new primitives beside ComponentPool unless every ECS form is refuted; ScratchColumn already expresses these lanes. The design's own critique found the FrameArena API cannot hold two lists at once: D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:468 "any UI system holding two lists do not compile". Retained capacity across frames (the UI's steady-state 0-alloc property) is native to ScratchColumn; a frame arena re-reserves every frame. Not decided here - flagged.
- **PC2** *rows:* UI text AST / lower / reconcile / serialize B rows; .keys load and save rows *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:361 "UI text AST/lower/report" (sent to HeapVec / HeapString with fmt::Write, rung 4) *plan_b:* this ledger: system-scratch on typed ScratchColumns owned by the loader system; strings as spans into the source; fmt::Write on ScratchBuildView&lt;u8> (KF6) *why_flagged:* HeapVec relocates on growth and is a second container vocabulary; the load runs inside ECS systems, so system-scratch applies. The two agree on diagnostics carrying codes.
- **PC3** *rows:* boyko_input/src/action/map.rs:134/136, row boyko_input/src/action/state.rs:61-67, raw/queue.rs:35 *plan_a:* D:/wt/joltab/docs/ARCH-AUDIT-ECS-DATA-REMEDIATION.md:9 "boyko_input (singleton state in `Resource`s as `Box<[T]>` by action-id)" - listed LEGIT *plan_b:* owner order 2026-09-10 (all runtime data onto our system, arrays onto the ECS) -> resource-column / event *why_flagged:* The audit predates the order; the order supersedes it, but the audit text still says LEGIT.
- **PC4** *rows:* boyko_ui/src/text/font.rs:31-41/139, world/pick.rs:106-121, boyko_input/src/raw/queue.rs:35 *plan_a:* D:/wt/joltab/docs/MEMORY-SYSTEM-AUDIT.md:140 "\| FontEntry/FontTable glyph tables \|", D:/wt/joltab/docs/MEMORY-SYSTEM-AUDIT.md:141 "\| UiWorldScratch \|" and D:/wt/joltab/docs/MEMORY-SYSTEM-AUDIT.md:145 "\| RawInputQueue.buf \|", each with verdict "**OK-by-design**" *plan_b:* this ledger: resource-column (fonts), system-scratch (pick lanes), event (raw input) *why_flagged:* Audited under the pre-order principle (Vec acceptable when frame-transient or fixed); superseded by the order but not retracted in the audit.
- **PC5** *rows:* boyko_ui/src/resources.rs:216/218/226 *plan_a:* D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-RESEARCH.md:698 "a CSR (offsets + flat) column pair" and D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:343 "CSR `FrameVec<u32>` offsets + flat `FrameVec<Entity>` two-pass" *plan_b:* this ledger: LIFO segments on one column per role (no two-pass) *why_flagged:* Minor: both are one reservation per role; the CSR two-pass needs a counting pass the LIFO shape does not, because the pools are dead after their frame returns.

Not an ECS-form question: diagnostics move to the engine's ONE diagnostics channel, boyko_log (structured record = a code from boyko_log/src/codes.rs + tagged args via its LogValue payload), so the message needs no heap String and the `Result<_, String>` returns become `Result<_, DiagCode>` with line/col. DiagArena refuted (primitives_refuted R5). If a consumer of the retained report exists (UiHotReload::last_report is read by a host after a reload), the ECS form of the retained records is an event (UiParseDiagnostic { line, col, code }) produced by the loader system.  [rev2 GAP 6] ecs_form diagnostics (new explicit form, decided by performance): cold diagnostic text and error payloads, built only on error / report paths, so the cost that matters is I-cache and the emitting thread's time, not allocation speed. Home, chosen per site: a &'static str or structured code (no bytes at all), a boyko_log ring record with formatting deferred to the drain (off the emitting thread), or a cold VmReservation-backed byte arena for a payload that must outlive the call; the recount's memory destination (DspBuf / InlineStr / DiagArena / KF-10 structured error) names which. Not kernel-internal: it is not the kernel's storage bookkeeping (most of these rows are in boyko_app and boyko_render), and moving it out lets the gate on kernel-internal close. Not out of scope: our code allocates it. KF-44 gives fn(&mut EcsMaster) bodies the same Local&lt;T> plus (last_run, this_run), which the engine design already names (main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DESIGN.md:894 "\| EK2 \| Exclusive system context \| `(last_run, this_run)` + `Local<S>` for exclusive bodies \| UI KF-A; services KF3 \| `ui_bind_apply` \| here \|").
